Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are fundamentally bottlenecked by the scarcity of expert demonstrations -- triplets of observations, instructions, and actions that are costly to collect at scale. We argue that this bottleneck stems from conflating two distinct learning objectives: acquiring physical competence (how to move) and acquiring semantic alignment (what to do). Crucially, only the latter requires language supervision. Building on this Decomposition Hypothesis, we propose Task-Agnostic Pretraining (TAP), a two-stage framework that first learns transferable motor priors from cheap, unlabeled interaction data -- including discarded off-task trajectories and autonomous robot play -- via a self-supervised Inverse Dynamics objective. A lightweight second stage then grounds these priors in language using minimal expert data. On the SIMPLER benchmark, TAP matches models trained on over 1M expert trajectories while using orders of magnitude less labeled data, yielding a 10% absolute gain over standard behavior cloning. On a real-world WidowX platform, TAP retains 25% success under camera perturbations where internet-scale baselines collapse to 0%, demonstrating that task-agnostic pretraining produces robust, transferable physical representations and offers a scalable path forward for Embodied AI.

</details>


### [130] [Learning Agile Intruder Interception using Differentiable Quadrotor Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02472)
*Michael Anoruo,Xiaoyu Tian,Abhishek Rathod,Timothy Naudet,Thomas Canchola,Eric Sturzinger,Kshitij Goel,Wennie Tabib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅使用3D方向单位向量和拦截器状态进行无人机拦截的学习方法，利用可微四旋翼动力学分析策略梯度，在10m/s速度下比基线方法性能提升30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习方法需要相对位置或距离信息，但在仅配备被动单目相机传感器的真实场景中难以获取这些信息。

Method: 采用可微四旋翼动力学的分析策略梯度方法，仅使用三维方向单位向量和拦截器状态学习拦截策略。

Result: 在高达10m/s的速度下实现敏捷拦截，比使用简化质点动力学的基线方法平均提升30%。

Conclusion: 所提方法在仅使用方向信息的条件下有效实现了高速拦截任务，显著优于传统的简化动力学方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a methodology for learning a control policy to intercept an intruder using the 3D direction unit vector to the intruder and the interceptor state. Prior deep reinforcement learning approaches assume either relative position or distance to the intruder is available, but this information is not readily accessible in real-world applications that employ passive, monocular camera sensors. Instead, we propose a solution that leverages an analytical policy gradient method using differentiable quadrotor dynamics to learn agile interception at speeds up to 10 m/s. The proposed approach outperforms baseline methods that utilize simplified point mass dynamics by an average of 30%.

</details>


### [131] [QuadRocket: An Aerial Robotic Testbed for Adaptive Thrust-Vector Control of Rocket-Like Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02474)
*Pedro Santos,Joel Reis,Paulo Oliveira,Carlos Silvestre*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出QuadRocket——一种基于四旋翼的火箭原型平台，通过万向节将圆柱体连接在四旋翼上方构成倒立摆系统。采用轴对称刚体模型、降阶姿态表示及自适应反步控制器实现近乎全局的轨迹跟踪与未知扰动补偿，并通过控制点变换缓解非最小相位行为。仿真与室内实验验证了其作为推力矢量控制测试平台的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有火箭推力矢量控制策略验证成本高、风险大，缺乏低成本、低风险的实验测试平台。

Method: 1) 将四旋翼与圆柱体耦合系统建模为单轴对称刚体，采用降阶姿态表示（二维球面）解耦偏航与推力矢量方向；2) 设计自适应反步控制器实现未知常值扰动下的轨迹跟踪，通过控制点变换缓解非最小相位行为；3) 将四旋翼作为推力矢量执行器，设计基于动态面的姿态控制器追踪期望推力矢量，避免虚拟控制信号的显式微分。

Result: 仿真与室内运动捕捉实验验证了系统能实现精确轨迹跟踪和有效扰动补偿，确认QuadRocket作为推力矢量控制测试平台的适用性。

Conclusion: QuadRocket为推力矢量控制策略验证提供了一种低成本、低风险的通用测试平台，具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: This paper presents QuadRocket, a quadrotor-based rocket prototype that provides a low-cost, low-risk platform for validating advanced thrust-vector control strategies for launch vehicle-type systems. The prototype consists of a cylindrical main body mounted on top of a quadrotor through a universal joint, forming a flying inverted pendulum with non-negligible inertia. For control design, the coupled system is modeled as a single axisymmetric rigid body actuated by a vectored force applied along its longitudinal axis. A reduced-attitude representation on the two sphere is adopted to explicitly exploit the vehicle's axial symmetry and to decouple yaw from the thrust-vector direction. On this model, we derive an adaptive backstepping controller that achieves almost global trajectory tracking in the presence of unknown constant disturbances, while a control-point transformation mitigates non minimum-phase behavior. The quadrotor is then treated as a thrust vector actuator, and a dynamic-surface-based attitude controller is designed to track the desired thrust-vector, accounting for actuation dynamics and avoiding explicit differentiation of virtual control signals. The complete architecture is evaluated in simulation and validated experimentally in an indoor motion-capture arena. Results demonstrate accurate trajectory tracking, effective disturbance compensation, and confirm the suitability of the QuadRocket as a versatile testbed for thrust-vector-controlled robotic vehicles.

</details>


### [132] [Controllable Sim Agents with Behavior Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02496)
*Juanwu Lu,Junyu Zhu,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出可控神经变分智能体（CNeVA），一种可控制的仿真智能体框架，通过闭式共轭变分更新从每通道折扣回报中推断每智能体高斯行为潜变量，并基于混合通道掩码课程的条件整流流轨迹生成器实现分类器无关引导。在Waymo开放运动数据集上，CNeVA在保证竞争性真实感的同时，提供了排名更高的模仿模型所缺乏的每通道可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实交通仿真需要既能模仿记录行为、又能沿可解释轴进行控制的智能体。这种可控性使工程师能够隔离变量、复现特定边缘案例并在无真实世界风险下测试自动驾驶系统。

Method: 1) 通过闭式共轭变分更新从每通道折扣回报中推断每智能体高斯行为潜变量；2) 采用混合通道掩码课程训练的分类器无关引导整流流轨迹生成器；3) 提出软资格门控（soft eligibility gates），以平滑指数衰减替代硬二进制阈值，保留近阈值智能体的梯度信号。

Result: 在Waymo数据集上，CNeVA在基准测试中达到竞争性真实感，同时展现每通道可控性。速度和加速度控制产生单调响应且无失速导致的奖励黑客行为。引入软资格门控后，安全可控性呈单调且显著提升。在上下文残差回报度量下实现了可控地图合规性。

Conclusion: 可控指标必须与物理合理性护栏一同解读，以避免奖励黑客混淆。CNeVA框架为交通仿真提供了可解释的控制维度，弥补了高排名模仿模型在可控性方面的不足。

Abstract: Realistic traffic simulation requires agents that imitate logged behavior and can also be steered along interpretable axes. Such controllability enables engineers to isolate variables, reproduce specific edge cases, and test autonomous systems without real-world risk. We introduce Controllable Neural Variational Agents (CNeVA), a controllable simulated-agent framework that learns to infer a per-agent Gaussian behavior latent from per-channel discounted returns via a closed-form conjugate variational update, conditioning a rectified-flow trajectory generator trained on a mixed channel-mask curriculum for classifier-free guidance. To tackle scarcity in reward signals, we propose soft eligibility gates that replace hard binary thresholds with smooth exponential decay, preserving the gradient signal for near-threshold agents. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, CNeVA attains competitive realism on the benchmark while exposing per-channel controllability that the higher-ranked imitation models lack. Speed- and acceleration-based steering produces monotone responses without stall-induced reward hacking. Safety controllability is monotone and substantial with the introduction of soft eligibility. We manage to achieve steerable map compliance under a context-residual return measure. Furthermore, our experiment demonstrates that steering metrics must be read alongside physical-plausibility guardrails to avoid reward-hacking confounds.

</details>


### [133] [Embodied.cpp: A Portable Inference Runtime of Embodied AI Models on Heterogeneous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02501)
*Ling Xu,Chuyu Han,Borui Li,Hao Wu,Shiqi Jiang,Ting Cao,Chuanyou Li,Sheng Zhong,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Embodied.cpp 是一个便携式 C++ 推理运行时，专为具身AI模型设计，通过五层架构实现多速率执行、延迟优先融合推理和可扩展接口，在异构设备上高效部署VLA模型和世界-动作模型，实验表明在保持高精度的同时显著提升部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身AI模型（VLA和WAM）的部署分散在不同模型特定的Python栈和后端假设中，且现有推理运行时主要面向请求-响应模式，无法满足具身部署的运行时契约：闭环控制下的多速率执行、异构硬件上的延迟优先batch-1推理，以及超越固定token I/O的可扩展具身接口。

Method: 基于对代表性VLA模型和WAM的架构分析，提取共享执行路径并组织为五层架构：输入适配器、序列构建器、骨干执行、头插件和部署适配器。提供模块化多速率执行、延迟优先融合推理、可扩展的算子与I/O支持，通过单一后端抽象实现跨异构设备、机器人和模拟器的部署。

Result: 在两个VLA模型（HY-VLA和pi0.5）上实现闭环执行，任务成功率分别达100.0%和91.0%；在基于LingBot-VA Transformer块的初步WAM基准测试中，将块内存从312.2 MiB降至88.1 MiB。

Conclusion: Embodied.cpp在保持高精度的前提下，有效提升了不同具身模型架构的部署效率。

Abstract: Embodied AI models now span vision-language-action (VLA) models and world-action models (WAMs), but practical deployment remains fragmented across model-specific Python stacks, backend assumptions, and robot-side glue code, especially on heterogeneous edge devices. Existing inference runtimes are designed mainly for request-response serving and therefore do not satisfy the runtime contract of embodied deployment: multi-rate execution inside closed-loop control, latency-first batch-1 inference on heterogeneous hardware, and extensible embodied interfaces beyond fixed token I/O. We present Embodied.cpp, a portable C++ inference runtime for embodied models. Based on an architectural analysis of representative VLA models and WAMs, Embodied.cpp captures a shared execution path and organizes it into five layers: input adapters, sequence builders, backbone execution, head plugins, and deployment adapters. The runtime provides modular multi-rate execution, latency-first fused inference, and extensible operator and I/O support, enabling deployment across heterogeneous devices, robots, and simulators through one backend abstraction. We evaluate Embodied.cpp on two VLA models, HY-VLA and pi0.5, and on a preliminary WAM benchmark using a LingBot-VA Transformer block. The VLA deployments achieve successful closed-loop execution with 100.0% and 91.0% task success rates, respectively. The WAM benchmark reduces block memory from 312.2 MiB to 88.1 MiB. These results show that Embodied.cpp improves deployment efficiency while preserving high accuracy across diverse embodied model architectures.

</details>


### [134] [VT-WAM: Visual-Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02503)
*Shuai Tian,Yupeng Zheng,Yuhang Zheng,Songen Gu,Yujie Zang,Yuxing Qin,Weize Li,Haoran Li,Wenchao Ding,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VT-WAM是一个视觉-触觉世界动作模型，通过统一流匹配框架联合学习视觉预测、触觉变形预测和动作预测，在六项接触丰富操作任务中平均成功率达71.67%，优于基线方法26.67%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-触觉策略通常直接将触觉观测输入动作预测，很少在动作生成过程中建模触觉变形动态，而接触丰富操作恰恰需要策略对局部变形、压力、滑移和摩擦等时域稀疏且视觉难以捕捉的触觉线索做出反应。

Method: 提出VT-WAM框架：(1) 非对称混合Transformer (MoT) 注意力，桥接首帧视觉锚点与时域触觉动态；(2) 接触门控的动作-视觉-触觉注意力引导 (AVTAG)，在接触阶段鼓励动作查询依赖触觉证据。

Result: 在6项真实世界接触丰富操作任务中平均成功率71.67%，分别超越Fast-WAM 26.67%和OmniVTLA 35.84%。

Conclusion: 建模触觉变形动态和引导接触阶段触觉注意力对于接触丰富操作任务均至关重要。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to react to local deformation, pressure, slip, and friction, yet these cues are temporally sparse and often invisible in visual observations. Existing visual-tactile policies usually feed tactile observations directly into action prediction, but rarely model tactile deformation dynamics during action generation. In this paper, we introduce VT-WAM, a Visual-Tactile World Action Model that jointly learns future visual prediction, tactile deformation prediction, and action prediction within a unified flow matching framework. In particular, VT-WAM introduces (1) Asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention to bridge a first-frame visual anchor with temporal tactile dynamics, and (2) contact-gated Action-Visual-Tactile Attention Guidance (AVTAG) to encourage action queries to rely on tactile evidence during contact phases. Across six real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, VT-WAM achieves a 71.67% average success rate, outperforming Fast-WAM by 26.67% and OmniVTLA by 35.84%. Ablations demonstrate that modeling tactile deformation dynamics and guiding contact-phase tactile attention are both important for contact-rich tasks. Project website: https://vt-wam.github.io/.

</details>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Parent Hamiltonians of Ergodic Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01446)
*Owen Ekblad,Eloy Moreno-Nadales,Eric B. Roon,Jeffrey H. Schenker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由站点依赖随机张量定义的遍历矩阵乘积态（EMPS）的母哈密顿量，证明了其热力学极限是整个自旋链上唯一无挫基态，但该哈密顿量不一定有能隙，并利用鞅方法给出了有能隙的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将矩阵乘积态（MPS）的母哈密顿量理论从平移不变情形推广到统计平移不变的遍历矩阵乘积态（EMPS），研究其母哈密顿量的存在性、性质（如有限程性、能隙）以及热力学极限行为。

Method: 采用鞅方法（martingale method）并结合局域统计量分析，在温和的单射性假设下证明母哈密顿量的存在性，并推导能隙存在的条件。

Result: 证明了EMPS的热力学极限是整个自旋链上唯一无挫基态；母哈密顿量可能有限程也可能非有限程；与平移不变情形不同，这些哈密顿量不一定是gapped的；通过鞅方法给出了gap存在的条件，并给出了有能隙和无能隙的示例。

Conclusion: EMPS的母哈密顿量理论比平移不变MPS更丰富——基态唯一且无挫，但能隙非必然；鞅方法结合局域统计是分析此类随机系统的有效工具。

Abstract: Matrix product states (MPS) are quintessential examples of frustration-free gapped ground states of local interactions called parent Hamiltonians. In this work, we investigate parent Hamiltonians for a class of ergodic matrix product states (EMPS), which are MPS defined by site-dependent random tensors $\{X_j^{[k]}\}_{j=1}^D$ which are homogeneously distributed at every site $k$ in the spin chain. Here, the EMPS are not translation-invariant but rather statistically translation-invariant. Under a mild injectivity assumption, we show the thermodynamic limit of an EMPS is the unique frustration-free ground state of a parent Hamiltonian on the whole spin chain, which, depending on the statistical properties of the EMPS, may or may not be finite-range. In contrast to the translation-invariant regime, these Hamiltonians need not be gapped. Nevertheless, applying the martingale method while keeping track of local statistics gives conditions for a gap, in addition to pointing towards why there need not be a gap in general. We include examples of EMPS both with and without spectral gaps to illustrate our results.

</details>


### [136] [Small Denominators and Subresonant Accumulation in Weakly Nonlinear Dispersive Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01447)
*P. Yu. Astafieva,O. M. Kiselev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本论文研究了弱非线性色散动力学中的小分母机制，提出"次共振累积"概念，分析非共振傅里叶相互作用族因失谐趋于零而导致的时间幂律增长效应，并在Klein-Gordon方程的四次傅里叶族中验证了该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱非线性色散方程经傅里叶分解后变为无穷弱耦合振子系统，高阶修正项中包含无限族非共振傅里叶相互作用，其失谐量趋于零。这类相互作用虽不产生精确久期项，但其累积贡献可能随时间幂次增长，需要系统分析这一"次共振累积"效应。

Method: 建立了受迫振子模型和抽象次共振Duhamel和的严格分析框架。当失谐量Δ_n ~ c n^{-p}且系数B_n ~ b n^{-κ}时，推导出累积贡献按t^{1-α}增长，其中α=(κ-1)/p。随后在Klein-Gordon色散律的四次傅里叶族中展示该机制的具体实现。

Result: 给出了次共振累积效应的定量增长规律：累积贡献的时间增长指数为1-α，α由失谐和系数的衰减指数决定。在Klein-Gordon方程中确认了该机制的存在，并给出了条件性近似结果：若所有剩余共振和近共振相互作用被控制，则次共振项主导长时间修正。

Conclusion: 揭示了弱非线性色散系统中一类新的长时间增长机制——次共振累积，为理解非共振相互作用在长时间尺度上的累积效应提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We study a small-denominator mechanism in weakly nonlinear dispersive dynamics. After Fourier decomposition, a nonlinear dispersive equation becomes an infinite system of weakly coupled oscillators. Higher-order correction terms may then contain infinite families of nonresonant Fourier interactions whose detunings tend to zero. Such families do not produce exact secular terms, but their accumulated contribution may grow as a power of time. We call this effect subresonant accumulation. The rigorous part of the paper is the analysis of a model forced oscillator and of an abstract subresonant Duhamel sum. If the detuning and coefficients have the form $Δ_n\sim c n^{-p}$ and $B_n\sim b n^{-κ}$, then the accumulated contribution grows as $t^{1-α}$, where $α=(κ-1)/p$. We then show how this mechanism appears in a quartic Fourier family for the Klein--Gordon dispersion law. For the full nonlinear partial differential equation we formulate a conditional approximation result: provided that all remaining resonant and almost resonant interactions are controlled, the subresonant term gives the leading long-time correction.

</details>


### [137] [$r$-deformed $α$-$z$-Rényi relative entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01805)
*Srikrishna Maity,Shigeru Furuichi,Supriyo Dutta*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类三参数Rényi相对熵族——$r$-形变$\alpha$-$z$-Rényi相对熵，证明了其满足散度的必要公理，确定了数据处理不等式成立的参数范围，并发现该新量是Tsallis相对熵的更紧上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广现有的$\alpha$-$z$-Rényi相对熵，引入第三个参数$r$以构建更一般的相对熵族，并寻找Tsallis相对熵的更紧上界。

Method: 通过$r$-对数定义三参数Rényi相对熵族，验证其散度公理，分析数据处理不等式成立的参数条件($\alpha, z, r$范围)，并比较不同上界的紧致性。

Result: $r$-形变$\alpha$-$z$-Rényi相对熵满足散度公理；给出了数据处理不等式成立的参数范围；该新量是Tsallis相对熵的上界，且比文献中已有的上界更紧。

Conclusion: $r$-形变$\alpha$-$z$-Rényi相对熵是Tsallis相对熵的一个更紧的上界，在密度算子适用时具有更好的紧致性。

Abstract: In this article, we consider the $r$-logarithm for defining three-parameter family of Rényi relative entropies that are generalization of the $α$-$z$-Rényi relative entropies. All the members of $r$-deformed $α$-$z$-Rényi relative entropies satisfy the necessary axioms to be a divergence. We expose the range of parameters $α$, $z$ and $r$ for which the data processing inequality holds. We also establish that $r$-deformed $α$-$z$-Rényi relative entropy is an upper bound of the Tsallis relative entropy. Now, we have two upper bounds of the Tsallis relative entropy, which are $r$-deformed $α$-$z$-Rényi relative entropy and the other one, which is discussed in literature. We investigate the order relationship between these two upper bounds of the Tsallis relative entropy. We observe that our new upper bound is more tighter when applicable to the density operators.

</details>


### [138] [Open-boundary integrable quantum circuits with different geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02093)
*Miguel García Fernández,Chiara Paletta,Ana L. Retore*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对具有开放边界条件和任意电路几何结构的可积Yang-Baxter量子电路进行了完整分类，提出了检测可积性的算法，并证明了最小电路深度为4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对可积量子电路的研究主要集中在周期边界条件，而开放边界条件和任意几何结构的可积电路分类尚不完整，缺乏系统的理论框架。

Method: 基于标准传输矩阵构造，引入两类交错非均匀性，推导出电路门排列与非均匀性及系统大小的映射关系；引入第三类非均匀性ρ，分析其在端点和邻近位置的配置效应。

Result: 证明了当局部体门和边界门满足Yang-Baxter方程且每个最近邻自旋对每周期恰好应用一次相同体门时，时间周期量子电路是可积的；开发了检测任意几何电路Yang-Baxter可积性的算法；证明了最小电路深度为4；给出了6-顶点和8-顶点R矩阵对应的两量子比特门及其反射矩阵的构造实例。

Conclusion: 该构造是完全通用的，适用于差分型和非差分型正规R矩阵及其关联的边界矩阵，为可积量子电路的分类和设计提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We present a complete classification of integrable Yang-Baxter quantum circuits with open boundary conditions and arbitrary circuit geometries. Starting from the standard transfer-matrix construction with two types of staggered inhomogeneities, we derive a general mapping that determines the arrangement of circuit gates in terms of the inhomogeneities and the system size. We conjecture that time-periodic quantum circuits are integrable whenever the local bulk and boundary gates satisfy the Yang-Baxter equation and the same bulk gate is applied exactly once per period to every nearest-neighbor pair of spins. Our construction also provides an algorithm to detect Yang-Baxter integrability for circuits with arbitrary geometries. Furthermore, we introduce a third type of inhomogeneity, denoted by $ρ$, and demonstrate that the minimum possible circuit depth is four. We show that when these $ρ$-inhomogeneities are placed at the endpoints and in their immediate neighborhood, the resulting boundary gates can be interpreted as single gates acting on multiple sites. Our construction is fully general and applies to regular $R$-matrices, both of difference and non-difference type, together with their associated boundary matrices. As an application, we consider two-qubit gates corresponding to 6- and 8-vertex $R$-matrices of non-difference form satisfying the Yang-Baxter equation, and we construct the associated reflection matrices that generate integrable quantum circuits.

</details>


### [139] [Mean-Field Bose--Einstein Condensation and Condensate Ideals in the Resolvent Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02264)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非理想玻色气体在Kac密度律和平均场Euler方程选择出具有正零模超额凝聚密度后的BEC相。选定的化学势抵消了平均场位移，使得单粒子哈密顿量回归自由形式。通过零模协方差在预解代数中定义平均场BEC理想态，并利用占据数和布朗环公式再现了密度选择、超额密度、ODLRO、局域检验等结果，同时区分了有限密度BEC与Buchholz严格无限占据的proper-condensate判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Kac密度律和平均场Euler方程下非理想玻色气体的BEC性质，特别是零模超额凝聚密度的选择机制及其数学结构。

Method: 采用预解代数框架分析零模协方差，定义平均场BEC理想态；运用占据数形式和布朗环公式进行双重验证；通过非正则商和直积分中心记录表示论数据。

Result: 化学势抵消平均场位移后，单粒子哈密顿量退化为自由形式；占据数和布朗环两种形式化方法均一致地导出密度选择、超额密度、ODLRO和局域检验结果；明确了有限密度BEC与Buchholz无限占据proper-condensate判据的分离。

Conclusion: 在Kac密度律和平均场Euler方程描述的BEC体系中，平均场位移被完全抵消，系统呈现自由玻色子行为；通过多种形式化方法的一致性验证，建立了该BEC态的完整数学描述，并厘清了不同BEC判据之间的边界。

Abstract: This paper studies the imperfect Bose gas after the Kac density law and the mean-field Euler equations have selected a condensed density with positive zero-mode excess. In this BEC regime the selected chemical potential cancels the mean-field shift, so the selected one-particle Hamiltonian is exactly the free one. The resulting zero-mode covariance defines a mean-field BEC ideal in the resolvent algebra, while the nonregular quotient and the direct-integral center record distinct representation-theoretic data. Occupation-number and Brownian-loop formulations recover the same density selection, excess density, ODLRO data, local tests, and the separation between finite-density BEC and Buchholz's stricter infinite-occupation proper-condensate criterion.

</details>


### [140] [Mesoscopic Linear Statistics for Two Ensembles of Quantum Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02356)
*Anna Maltsev,Mohammed Osman*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种随机量子图系综的介观线性谱统计，证明在大图极限下，多项式介观尺度上的线性谱统计量的方差与高斯正交/酉系综一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究随机量子图（由离散正则图和随机酉矩阵构造）的谱统计性质，验证其在介观尺度上是否展现与经典随机矩阵系综（GOE/GUE）相同的普适行为。

Method: 定义两类随机量子图系综：(1) 离散图从d-正则图中均匀采样；(2) 顶点邻域酉矩阵从Haar测度中独立均匀采样。分析大图极限下多项式介观尺度上线性谱统计量的方差，并与GOE/GUE进行比较。

Result: 证明了两类随机量子图系综在介观尺度上的线性谱统计量方差与GOE/GUE的方差一致。

Conclusion: 随机量子图在介观尺度上展现出与经典随机矩阵系综相同的谱统计普适性，进一步扩展了随机矩阵理论在量子图系统中的应用边界。

Abstract: We study mesoscopic linear spectral statistics for two ensembles of random quantum graphs. These are defined by a discrete graph $G$ and a unitary-matrix-valued function $U(k)$ indexed by directed edges of $G$. The matrix function $U(k)$ is constructed from unitary matrices $U^{(v)}$ indexed by the neighbours of each vertex $v$. The first ensemble is obtained by sampling the underlying discrete graph uniformly from the set of $d$-regular graphs. The second ensemble is obtained by sampling $U^{(v)}$ uniformly from the Haar measure, independently for each vertex. We prove that the variance of a linear spectral statistic in the large graph limit on polynomial mesoscopic scales coincides with that of the Gaussian Orthogonal/Unitary Ensemble.

</details>


### [141] [On a Rosenzweig-Porter-type model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02446)
*Giorgio Cipolloni,László Erdős,Joscha Henheik*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一般化Rosenzweig-Porter模型H=H₀+λW，其中H₀为任意厄米矩阵，W为Wigner矩阵，精确刻画了特征向量局域化性质和本征态热化假说(ETH)随耦合常数λ变化的转变过程，并证明了在任意H₀和λ下的一致结果，推广了以往变形Wigner矩阵的局部律，发现了迁移率边和再入局域化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变形Wigner矩阵的局部律仅适用于平均场区域，缺乏对一般非平均场情形的统一处理；此外，对于Rosenzweig-Porter模型中特征向量局域化转变及本征态热化假说的精确刻画仍不完善，需要建立在整个参数范围内一致成立的理论框架。

Method: 采用解析方法，精确推导了具有强非均匀结构的预解式的确定性近似；通过对一般化Rosenzweig-Porter模型进行严格的谱分析，在任意H₀和λ下建立一致的局部律，从而刻画特征向量局域化性质和ETH的转变。

Result: 获得了在任意H₀和λ下一致成立的局部律，显著推广了以往仅适用于平均场区域的结果；精确描述了预解式的确定性近似及其强非均匀结构；发现了迁移率边的出现；揭示了再入局域化现象。

Conclusion: 本文建立的一般化理论框架为理解无序系统中局域化-退局域化转变提供了统一视角，迁移率边和再入局域化的发现对相关物理系统的理解具有重要意义。

Abstract: We consider a very general Rosenzweig-Porter-type model, $H=H_0+λW$, where $H_0$ is an arbitrary Hermitian matrix and $W$ is a standard Wigner matrix. We precisely trace the localization properties of the eigenvectors and the eigenstate thermalisation hypothesis (ETH) as the coupling constant $λ$ interpolates between the trivial $λ=0$ case and the fully mean field regime of large $λ$. Our results hold uniformly in $H_0$ and $λ$, substantially generalising all previous local laws on deformed Wigner matrices even in the mean field regime. Our proof precisely captures the deterministic approximation to the resolvent which exhibits a strongly inhomogeneous structure. As a byproduct, we conclude the emergence of a mobility edge and study the phenomenon of re-entrant localization.

</details>

### [142] [Study of ZZ and ZH production in the bb$ττ$ final state and search for high-mass spin-0 and spin-1 resonances in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01589)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于CMS实验在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中收集的138 fb$^{-1}$数据，研究了ZZ和ZH过程在bbττ末态的生成。进行了非共振分析（针对标准模型ZZ和ZH过程）和共振分析（搜索超出标准模型的重粒子X→ZZ和Z'→ZH）。在95%置信水平上设置了生产截面与分支比乘积的上限，未观察到与标准模型预期的偏差。这是首次对ZZ/ZH→bbττ过程的测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究由非共振希格斯玻色子对产生搜索中bbττ通道的重要作用所驱动，同时扩展了对超出标准模型的重自旋0和自旋1共振粒子的搜索范围。

Method: 利用CMS探测器在LHC上收集的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据，积分亮度138 fb$^{-1}$，分析bbττ末态。针对非共振ZZ和ZH过程以及共振衰变X→ZZ（质量0.2-5 TeV）和Z'→ZH（质量0.5-6 TeV）进行搜索。

Result: 对σ(X)B(X→ZZ)设置上限为300 pb到24 fb，对σ(Z')B(Z'→ZH)设置上限为0.4 pb到12 fb（95%置信水平）。未观察到偏离标准模型预期的信号。

Conclusion: 这是首次对ZZ/ZH→bbττ过程进行实验探测，结果与标准模型一致，并对超出标准模型的新物理设定了限制。

Abstract: A study of the production of pairs of Z bosons (ZZ) and of the associated production of a Z boson and a Higgs boson (ZH) in final states containing two b quarks and two tau leptons (bb$ττ$) is presented. The analysis is based on proton-proton collisions collected at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV by the CMS experiment at the LHC, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. The nonresonant analysis targets the standard model ZZ and ZH processes in the bb$ττ$ final state, motivated by the prominent role of this channel in searches for nonresonant Higgs boson pair production. The resonant searches target physics beyond the standard model, probing heavy spin-0 resonances X that decay into ZZ and spin-1 resonances Z' that decay into ZH, with masses in the 0.2$-$5 and 0.5$-$6 TeV ranges, respectively. Upper limits at 95% confidence level are set on the product of production rate and branching fraction $σ$(X)$\mathcal{B}$(X $\to$ ZZ), ranging from 300 pb to 24 fb, and $σ$(Z')$\mathcal{B}$(Z' $\to$ ZH), ranging from 0.4 pb to 12 fb. These are the first measurements to probe the ZZ/ZH $\to$ bb$ττ$ processes. No deviation from standard model expectations is observed.

</details>


### [143] [Heavy-Flavor Electron Classification Using Hadronic Environment as Point Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02225)
*Jingyu Zhang,Wanbing He,Long Ma*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用点云表示和多种集合机器学习架构（包括Transformer）区分重味夸克（粲和底）半轻子衰变产生的电子，发现模型表现主要受限于粲和底强子结构的固有相似性，而非模型表达能力。在约40%效率的工作点下，分类器纯度接近80%，显著优于手工构造的BDT基线。模型主要学习强子环境的几何和拓扑特征，且能捕捉超越手工变量之外的有效鉴别信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能碰撞中来自粲(D)和底(B)强子半轻子衰变的电子是重要探针，但两者衰变拓扑结构相似，传统方法难以有效区分。

Method: 将强子环境表示为点云，采用基于集合的机器学习架构（包括Transformer）进行粲/底起源电子分类，并与手工构造的BDT基线比较，通过特征扰动和物理可观测量关联分析模型学习到的表示。

Result: 不同架构性能相当，表明瓶颈在于数据本身而非模型。在40%效率工作点下纯度约80%，优于BDT基线。模型主要依赖强子环境的几何和拓扑特征，且能学习到超越手工变量的鉴别信息。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了粲/底电子区分能力，揭示了问题的根本限制因素，并为未来高能物理实验中的重味物理分析提供了新思路。

Abstract: Electrons from semi-leptonic decays of charm (D) and bottom (B) hadrons are important probes in high-energy collisions, while their separation remains challenging due to the similarity of the underlying decay topologies. In this work, we represent the hadronic environment as a point cloud and investigate a hadron-based approach for distinguishing charm- and bottom-origin electrons using several set-based machine learning architectures, including Transformer models. Comparable performance is observed across different architectures, indicating that the dominant limitation originates from the intrinsic similarity between charm- and bottom-related hadronic structures rather than model expressivity. At an experimentally relevant working point corresponding to approximately 40% efficiency, the classifier achieves a purity close to 80% on the test dataset and significantly improves the classification performance relative to a hand-crafted observable BDT baseline. By studying the relation between the model response and physics-motivated observables, together with feature perturbation tests, we find that the learned representation is primarily sensitive to geometric and topological properties of the hadronic environment. Comparisons with high-level observables further suggest that the learned representation captures nontrivial discriminating information beyond a small set of manually constructed variables.

</details>


### [144] [Measurement of solar $pp$ neutrino flux with the new PandaX-4T data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02405)
*PandaX Collaboration,Peiyuan Chen,Wei Chen,Xiaohua Chen,Xun Chen,Yunhua Chen,Chen Cheng,Xiangyi Cui,Yuxin Cui,Manna Deng,Roni Dey,Yingjie Fan,Deqing Fang,Xuanye Fu,Zhixing Gao,Yujie Ge,Lisheng Geng,Xunan Guo,Xuyuan Guo,Zichao Guo,Chencheng Han,Ke Han,Changda He,Jinrong He,Ruquan Hou,Houqi Huang,Junting Huang,Yule Huang,Xiangdong Ji,Yonglin Ju,Xiaorun Lan,Chenxiang Li,Mingchuan Li,Peiyuan Li,Shuaijie Li,Tao Li,Yangdong Li,Yuan Li,Zhiyuan Li,Qing Lin,Jianglai Liu,Yuanchun Liu,Yunyang Luo,Yugang Ma,Yajun Mao,Yue Meng,Binyu Pang,Ningchun Qi,Xiangxiang Ren,Dong Shan,Xiyuan Shao,Manbin Shen,Wenliang Sun,Xuyan Sun,Yi Tao,Yueqiang Tian,Yuxin Tian,Anqing Wang,Guanbo Wang,Hao Wang,Haoyu Wang,Jiamin Wang,Lei Wang,Meng Wang,Qiuhong Wang,Shaobo Wang,Shibo Wang,Siguang Wang,Wei Wang,Xu Wang,Zhou Wang,Yuehuan Wei,Weihao Wu,Yuan Wu,Mengjiao Xiao,Xiang Xiao,Yuhan Xie,Kaizhi Xiong,Jianqin Xu,Yifan Xu,Binbin Yan,Xiyu Yan,Yong Yang,Shunyu Yao,Peihua Ye,Chunxu Yu,Zhe Yuan,Youhui Yun,Minzhen Zhang,Peng Zhang,Shibo Zhang,Shu Zhang,Siyuan Zhang,Tao Zhang,Wei Zhang,Yang Zhang,Yingxin Zhang,Yuanyuan Zhang,Kangkang Zhao,Li Zhao,Jiaxu Zhou,Jiayi Zhou,Jifang Zhou,Ning Zhou,Xiaopeng Zhou,Zhizhen Zhou,Chenhui Zhu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: PandaX-4T实验利用Run 2数据（曝光量1.9吨·年）测量太阳pp中微子通量，通过中微子-电子弹性散射在20-1000 keV能区进行盲分析，结合Run 0数据得到pp通量为(8.5 ± 3.5)×10^{10} cm^{-2}s^{-1}，在165 keV以下首次以2.2σ显著性表明太阳pp中微子-电子散射的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量太阳pp中微子通量以检验标准太阳模型，并探索在低电子反冲能量区域（<165 keV）探测pp中微子-电子散射的可能性。

Method: 基于PandaX-4T探测器Run 2数据，经过探测器升级、放射性惰性气体杂质约束（利用物理数据自身及气体分析系统）、数据处理链改进、探测器响应表征和背景模型优化后，对20-1000 keV电子反冲数据进行盲谱分析。

Result: 结合Run 0数据，拟合得到pp通量为(8.5 ± 3.5)×10^{10} cm^{-2}s^{-1}，与标准太阳模型预测一致；在165 keV以下以2.2σ显著性首次实现太阳pp中微子-电子散射的正向指示。

Conclusion: 该结果标志着在低能区（<165 keV）首次获得太阳pp中微子-电子散射的正向信号，为未来更精确的低能太阳中微子测量奠定了基础。

Abstract: We report a new measurement of the solar proton--proton ($pp$) neutrino flux via neutrino--electron elastic scattering using the PandaX-4T Run 2 data set collected between 2024 and 2026, corresponding to an exposure of 1.9 tonne$\cdot$yr. Before Run 2 data taking, the detector underwent a series of upgrades to improve its response and background conditions. Time variations of radioactive noble-gas impurities are constrained using the physics data themselves, complemented by measurements from the gas-assay system. The analysis introduced improvements in the data processing chain, detector response characterization, and background models. A blind spectral analysis was then performed on the electronic-recoil data across a wide energy range from 20 to 1000 keV. In combination with the Run 0 data published earlier, the fitted $pp$ flux is $(8.5 \pm 3.5)\times 10^{10}$ $\mathrm{cm^{-2}s^{-1}}$, consistent with the prediction of the Standard Solar Model. With a statistical significance of $2.2σ$ above background, this marks the first positive indication of solar $pp$ neutrino--electron scattering below an electronic-recoil energy of 165 keV.

</details>
